        <text id="5825:3925" name="What is “Paper Kit”?" x="100" y="80" width="348" height="60" />
        <text id="5825:3934" name="The Paper Kit is an accessible, low-fidelity Figma UI kit that allows both designers and non-designers to feel empowered to generate product concepts. It levels the playing field, helping cross-functional teams collaborate and express their thoughts more easily. The name Paper comes from the process of “paper prototyping” whereby the creator draws product screens quickly to represent rough ideas. These concepts are often put in front of real users and can be used to test your ideas early in the process. So, while this is not a physical representation of paper, it can have the same impact while also allowing you to use your favorite design tool; Figma! :)" x="100" y="164" width="494" height="480" />
      </frame>
    </frame>
